Use when a diagnosis, evaluation, intervention, growth change, policy, product change, or observational relationship depends on what caused what, and confounding, selection, timing, mediation, spillovers, or counterfactual choice could alter the conclusion. - **research-causal-graphs**: Deep-research a trait's causal mechanism against the literature and compare it to the trait's existing causal_graphs.
